      try {
        if (false) {
          var platform = document.documentElement.dataset.seedPlatform;

          if (platform === 'ios') {
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();
              } catch (e) {}
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyIOSFontScaling);
            } else {
              applyIOSFontScaling();
            }
          } else if (platform === 'android') {
            var fontSize = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
            var scale = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.5, fontSize / 16));
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(scale.toFixed(2)).toString();
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    })(window, document, 'system');

        if (false) {
          var platform = document.documentElement.dataset.seedPlatform;

          if (platform === 'ios') {
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();
              } catch (e) {}
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyIOSFontScaling);
            } else {
              applyIOSFontScaling();
            }
          } else if (platform === 'android') {
            var fontSize = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
            var scale = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.5, fontSize / 16));
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(scale.toFixed(2)).toString();
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    })(window, document, 'system');

            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();
              } catch (e) {}
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyIOSFontScaling);
            } else {
              applyIOSFontScaling();
            }
          } else if (platform === 'android') {
            var fontSize = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
            var scale = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.5, fontSize / 16));
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(scale.toFixed(2)).toString();
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    })(window, document, 'system');

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();
              } catch (e) {}
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyIOSFontScaling);
            } else {
              applyIOSFontScaling();
            }
          } else if (platform === 'android') {
            var fontSize = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
            var scale = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.5, fontSize / 16));
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(scale.toFixed(2)).toString();
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    })(window, document, 'system');

    (function(window, document, mode) {
      try {
        document.documentElement.dataset.seed = '';
        document.documentElement.dataset.seedColorMode = mode;
      } catch (e) {}

      try {
        var prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');

        if ('addEventListener' in prefersDark) {
          prefersDark.addEventListener('change', apply);
        } else if ('addListener' in prefersDark) {
          prefersDark.addListener(apply);
        }

        if (prefersDark.matches) {
          document.documentElement.dataset.seedUserColorScheme = 'dark';
        } else {
          document.documentElement.dataset.seedUserColorScheme = 'light';
        }

        function apply() {
          document.documentElement.dataset.seedUserColorScheme = prefersDark.matches ? 'dark' : 'light';
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      try {
        if (typeof window.AndroidFunction !== 'undefined') {
          document.documentElement.dataset.seedPlatform = 'android';
        } else if (typeof window.webkit !== 'undefined' && typeof window.webkit.messageHandlers !== 'undefined') {
          document.documentElement.dataset.seedPlatform = 'ios';
        } else {
          document.documentElement.dataset.seedPlatform = 'ios';
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      try {
        if (false) {
          var platform = document.documentElement.dataset.seedPlatform;

          if (platform === 'ios') {
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();
              } catch (e) {}
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyIOSFontScaling);
            } else {
              applyIOSFontScaling();
            }
          } else if (platform === 'android') {
            var fontSize = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
            var scale = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.5, fontSize / 16));
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(scale.toFixed(2)).toString();
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    })(window, document, 'system');

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();

            }

에릭입시영어 선생님들께서는 지친 아이들에게 항상 간식을 챙겨 주시며 에너지를 채워주시고, 작은 변화도 놓치지 않고 칭찬과 격려를 아끼지 않으십니다. 

특히 에릭쌤은 남자아이들이 좋아할 만한 유쾌한 입담과 재치가 있으셔서 아이도 선생님을 정말 좋아합니다.  
무엇보다 사춘기 스위치가 제대로 켜져 아이가 학원에 가지 않으려 하는 날에는 직접 연락하시고 찾아봐 주실 만큼, 한 명 한 명을 진심으로 챙겨주시는 모습이 인상 깊었습니다. 

덕분에 시험 점수도 30점 이상 향상됐고, 예전에는 학원 가기 싫다고 하던 아이가 지금은 정규수업이 아닌 재량수업인 금요일에도 빠지지 않고 스스로 가서 모르는 것을 질문하며 배우고 옵니다. 그 모습만 봐도 아이가 얼마나 즐겁게 다니는지 느껴지더라고요.

단순히 영어를 가르치는 곳이 아니라 아이의 성장을 지지해주시며 함께해 주는 학원이라 믿고 추천드립니다 ◡̈ !</span></div><div class="sprinkles_display_flex_base__1byufe82i sprinkles_gap_1.5_base__1byufe8s6 sprinkles_color_neutralSubtle__1byufe82 sprinkles_alignItems_center_base__1byufe8si"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" data-seed-icon="true" data-seed-icon-version="0.0.23" width="16" height="16"><g><path fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd" d="M11.1784 2C10.35 2 9.67838 2.67157 9.67838 3.5V5.75796C9.67838 7.73242 8.07304 9.38598 6.15303 9.39582H4.09396C3.14362 9.39582 2.11913 9.90945 1.75564 10.994C1.44021 11.9352 1.0957 13.4737 1.0957 15.6979C1.0957 17.922 1.44021 19.4605 1.75564 20.4017C2.11913 21.4863 3.14362 21.9999 4.09396 21.9999H6.61483C6.62405 22.0001 6.6333 22.0003 6.64258 22.0003C6.65186 22.0003 6.66111 22.0001 6.67033 21.9999H15.8486C18.8383 21.9999 21.3719 19.7988 21.7897 16.8385L22.5641 11.3517C22.819 9.54598 21.4172 7.932

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();
              } catch (e) {}
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyIOSFontScaling);
            } else {
              applyIOSFontScaling();
            }
          } else if (platform === 'android') {

cument.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';

            function applyIOSFontScaling() {
              try {
                var tempEl = document.createElement('div');
                tempEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;visibility:hidden;font-size:16px;font:-apple-system-body;';
                document.body.appendChild(tempEl);
                var size = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tempEl).fontSize);
                document.body.removeChild(tempEl);
                var mult = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.35, (size / 16) * 0.9412));
                document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(mult.toFixed(2)).toString();
              } catch (e) {}
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyIOSFontScaling);
            } else {
              applyIOSFontScaling();
            }
          } else if (platform === 'android') {
            var fontSize = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
            var scale = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.5, fontSize / 16));
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontMultiplier = parseFloat(scale.toFixed(2)).toString();
            document.documentElement.dataset.seedFontScaling = 'enabled';
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    })(window, document, 'system');
